]
)

\displaystyle\overline{b}_{(m)}^{\overline{\alpha}\overline{\beta}}(g)=\left(\accentset{\circ}{K}^{\overline{\alpha}\overline{\beta}}-\accentset{\circ}{K}\overline{g}^{\overline{\alpha}\overline{\beta}}\right)\mathrm{vol}_{\overline{g}}\,,

\displaystyle\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}:=\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{EH}}=\iota_{\bar{V}}\mathrm{vol}_{\bar{g}}\,,

(4.2)

\displaystyle\overline{V}_{\!\overline{\alpha}}=-\jmath^{\alpha}_{\overline{\alpha}}\nu^{\beta}\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}g_{\alpha\beta}\,.

4.3
Space of solutions

Although the manifold we are considering has a boundary term, the boundary equations only involve the metric, and hence we recover the same result (
3.30
). This implies that the boundary only plays a role in the metric sector of the solution space
\mathrm{Sol}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{EH}}
(discussed in detail in
[
36
]
, where both Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions were considered). The same techniques used here apply if one considers other boundary terms to impose different boundary conditions.

4.4
Presymplectic form

We have seen that
\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}=\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{PT}}=\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{EH}}
. This together with (
3.8
) leads once again to (
3.32
).

5
TETRAD-HMS WITH BOUNDARY

5.1
The action

The tetrad-HMS action is defined as
\mathbb{S}_{\textrm{HMS}}^{(t)}:=\mathbb{S}_{\textrm{HMS}}^{(m)}\circ\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
where

\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widetilde{\omega}):=\Phi_{\mathrm{PT}}(e,\widetilde{\omega})\overset{\text{\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{Number}{CPSPT}{}{}]}}}{=}\Big(\eta_{IJ}e_{\mu}^{I}e_{\nu}^{J},E^{\beta}_{K}e^{J}_{\alpha}\big(\mathchoice{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}-\mathchoice{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}\big)\Big)=(g_{\mu\nu},\mathchoice{Q^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}{\alpha}}}}{Q^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}{\alpha}}}}{Q^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}{\alpha}}}}{Q^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}{\alpha}}}})\,,

As usual,
e^{I}_{\alpha}
are the tetrad
1
-forms,
E_{I}^{\alpha}
the dual cotetrad vector fields,
\eta_{IJ}
the internal Lorentz metric,
\widetilde{\omega}^{IJ}
is the generic
1
-form connection (no symmetries in the internal indices), and
\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{IJ}
is the Levi-Civita
1
-form connection associated with the metric
g_{\mu\nu}=\eta_{IJ}e^{I}_{\mu}e^{J}_{\nu}
.

\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
is almost a change of variables from tetrads and spin connections
(e,\widetilde{\omega})\in\mathcal{F}^{(t)}_{\textrm{HMS}}
to metrics and
3
-tensors
(g,Q)\in\mathcal{F}^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}}
: it is surjective but not injective. In fact,

\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widetilde{\omega})=\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e^{\prime},\widetilde{\omega}^{\prime})\,\longleftrightarrow\,\exists\text{\,local }\Psi\in SO(1,3)\ \ \begin{array}[]{|l}e^{\prime}_{I}=\mathchoice{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}e_{J}\\
\widetilde{\omega}_{I}^{{}^{\prime}\,J}=\mathchoice{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\widetilde{\omega}\mathchoice{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Psi^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Psi^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Psi^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Psi^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}+\mathchoice{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$ (5.1)

Moreover, it is not too hard to check that
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
is surjective.

Remark 5.2
.

Naively, one might think that the fact that
\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
is almost a change of variables implies that the results derived in the metric formalism can be easily transferred into the tetrad formalism. However, this is not the case since
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
involves derivatives of
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}e^{I}_{\alpha}
(due to the variation of
\mathchoice{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}:=e^{K}_{\alpha}\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}_{\!\mu}E^{\alpha}_{I}
). These derivatives will appear in the computation of the symplectic potentials and charges.

From the aforementioned definition of the tetrad-HMS action, we choose the following representatives as the tetrad-HMS Lagrangians

\displaystyle L^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widetilde{\omega}):=L^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}\circ\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widetilde{\omega})\,,

\displaystyle\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widetilde{\omega}):=\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}\circ\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widetilde{\omega})\,.\

(5.3)

In this case, it is not really useful to write the Lagrangian as the sum of the EH-term and some coupling terms (in analogy with (
3.1
)). It is better to split the
1
-form connection in its antisymmetric and symmetric parts in its internal indices,

\widetilde{\omega}_{IJ}=\widehat{\omega}_{IJ}+S_{IJ}\,,

and consider the equivalent variables
(e,\widehat{\omega},S)
. Following
[
36
,
35
]
, one obtains the following explicit expressions for these Lagrangians

\displaystyle L_{\mathrm{HMS}}^{(t)}(e,\widehat{\omega},S)=\frac{1}{2}H_{IJKL}\Big(\widehat{F}^{IJ}-\frac{\Lambda}{6}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}+\mathchoice{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}\wedge S^{MJ}\Big)\wedge e^{K}\wedge e^{L}

\displaystyle\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}(e,\widehat{\omega})=-\frac{1}{2}H_{IJKL}\left(2N^{I}\mathrm{d}N^{J}-\overline{\widehat{\omega}}{}^{IJ}\right)\wedge\overline{e}^{K}\wedge\overline{e}^{L}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{d}\overline{e}_{I}\wedge\overline{e}^{I}\hbox to0pt{\vbox to0pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \immediate;
\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\hbox to0pt{\vbox to0pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \immediate;
\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}

where
\mathchoice{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}_{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}_{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}_{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scripts ,
\widehat{F}_{IJ}=\mathrm{d}\widehat{\omega}_{IJ}+\widehat{\omega}_{IK}\wedge\mathchoice{\widehat{\omega}^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\widehat{\omega}^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\widehat{\omega}^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\widehat{\omega}^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
,
\overline{e}^{I}:=\jmath^{*}e^{I}
,
\overline{\widehat{\omega}}{}^{IJ}:=\jmath^{*}\widehat{\omega}^{IJ}
, and
N^{I}=\nu^{\alpha}e^{I}_{\alpha}
. Notice that if we set
S=0
we recover the Holst Lagrangian on the bulk

L_{\mathrm{HMS}}^{(t)}=L_{\mathrm{Holst}}^{(t)}+\frac{1}{2}H_{IJKL}\mathchoice{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}\wedge S^{MJ}\wedge e^{K}\wedge e^{L}

while the boundary term is the one defined in
[
37
]
(although in that reference it is derived by working from the beginning with a Lorentz connection i.e.
S=0
). Notice in particular that the tetrad-HMS action can be interpreted as the generalization of the Holst action for
GL(4)
connections.

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}-\jmath^{*}\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}=\overline{b}^{(t)}_{I}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\overline{e}^{I}-\mathrm{d}\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}\,,

where the Euler-Lagrange equations are

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathfrak{E}^{(t)}_{L}&:=H_{IJKL}\Big(\widehat{F}^{IJ}+\mathchoice{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}\wedge S^{MJ}-\frac{\Lambda}{3}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\Big)\wedge e^{K}\,,\\
\mathcal{E}^{(t)}_{KL}&:=-\frac{1}{2}\widehat{D}(H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J})\,,\\
\mathscr{E}^{(t)}_{JM}&:=\frac{1}{2}\big(H_{IKLJ}\delta_{M}^{R}+H_{IKLM}\delta_{J}^{R}\big)\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{R}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{R}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{R}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{R}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\wedge e^{K}\wedge e^{L}\,,\\
\overline{b}^{(t)}_{I}&:=\epsilon_{IJKL}(2N^{K}\mathrm{d}N^{L}-\overline{\widehat{\omega}}{}^{KL})\wedge\overline{e}^{J}+2\epsilon_{MJKL}N^{L}(\iota_{\overline{E}^{J}}\mathrm{d}\overline{e}^{K})\wedge\overline{e}^{M}N_{I}-\frac{2}{\gamma}\widehat{D}\overline{e}_{I}\,,\end{split}

(5.4)

here
\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\alpha_{I}=\mathrm{d}\alpha_{I}+\mathchoice{\widehat{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\wedge\alpha_{J}
. We take the symplectic potentials

\displaystyle\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}:=\frac{1}{2}H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\widehat{\omega}^{KL}\,,

\displaystyle\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}:=\epsilon_{IJKL}\overline{e}^{I}\wedge\overline{e}^{J}\wedge N^{K}\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}N^{L}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\overline{e}^{I}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\overline{e}_{I}\,.

(5.5)

5.3
Space of solutions

One way to obtain the space of solutions is to prove that
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\Phi_{\mathrm{PT}}
is surjective. Then, because

\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}_{(e,\widetilde{\omega})}\mathbb{S}_{\textrm{HMS}}^{(t)}=\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}_{(e,\widetilde{\omega})}(\mathbb{S}_{\textrm{HMS}}^{(m)}\circ\Phi_{\mathrm{PT}})=\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}_{\Phi_{\mathrm{PT}}(e,\widetilde{\omega})}\mathbb{S}_{\textrm{HMS}}^{(m)}\circ\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}_{(e,\widetilde{\omega})}\Phi_{\mathrm{PT}}

(5.6)

(5.7)

Although it is not too hard to prove that
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\Phi_{\mathrm{PT}}
is surjective, here we will use the techniques of section
3.3
to solve
\mathscr{E}^{(t)}_{JM}=0
. To that purpose let us first expand
S_{IJ}=S_{MIJ}e^{M}
with
S_{MIJ}=S_{MJI}
to rewrite the third equation in (
5.4
) in the form

\epsilon_{KLNP}\mathchoice{H^{{{IKL}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.19345pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.13818pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.38525pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.41803pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(M}}}}{H^{{{IKL}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.19345pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.13818pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.38525pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.41803pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(M}}}}{H^{{{IKL}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.19345pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.13818pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.38525pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.41803pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(M}}}}{H^{{{IKL}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.50142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.19345pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.13818pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[16.78412pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.38525pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.41803pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(M}}}}\mathchoice{S^{{{N}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29794pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29794pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63242pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45172pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.08473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47124pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.19374pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J)I}}}}{S^{{{N}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29794pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[1 which is equivalent to

-2\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{I(M}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{I(M}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{I(M}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{I(M}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.20973pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.20973pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.09395pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.63853pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{P}}}_{{{J)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.16771pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.16771pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.82642pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.73315pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.20973pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.20973pt][c]{$\textstyle$} (5.8)

We use now the irreducible decomposition (
3.14
) (notice that we are allowed to do that despite the different “nature of the indices”) to write

S_{IJK}={}^{(1)}S_{IJK}+{}^{(2)}S_{IJK}+\Sigma_{IJK}+\sigma_{IJK}

(5.9)

with

\displaystyle{}^{(1)}S_{IJK}:=\frac{1}{4}A_{I}\eta_{JK}\,,

\displaystyle{}^{(2)}S_{IJK}:=\frac{1}{36}(\eta_{JK}\delta_{I}^{L}-2\eta_{IJ}\delta_{K}^{L}-2\eta_{IK}\delta_{J}^{L})(A_{L}-4B_{L})\,,

\displaystyle\Sigma_{IJK}=S_{(IJK)}-\frac{1}{18}(\eta_{IJ}\delta^{L}_{K}+\eta_{KI}\delta^{L}_{J}+\eta_{JK}\delta^{L}_{I})(A_{L}+2B_{L})\,,

\displaystyle\sigma_{IJK}:=S_{IJK}-{}^{(1)}S_{IJK}-{}^{(2)}S_{IJK}-\Sigma_{IJK}\,,

where all these tensors are symmetric in the last two indices,
\Sigma_{(IJK)}=\Sigma_{IJK}
,
\Sigma_{IJK}
and
\sigma_{IJK}
are traceless and, finally,
\sigma_{IJK}+\sigma_{KIJ}+\sigma_{JKI}=0
. We now solve (
5.9
) in steps. First, by contracting it with
\delta^{J}_{P}
we get

\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.82637pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.59026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{MI}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.82637pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.59026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{MI}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.82637pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.59026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{MI}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.82637pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.59026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{MI}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-4\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.82637pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.59026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{IM}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.46461pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox A_{M}-4B_{M}=0\,.

By symmetrizing (
5.9
) in the indices
MJP
we find

S_{(JMP)}-\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{I(M}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{I(M}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{I(M}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.58963pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.73192pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.95137pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{I(M}}}}^{I}\eta_{JP)}=0\qquad\longleftrightarrow\qquad\Sigma_{JMP}+\frac{1}{6}(A_{(J}-4B_{(J})\eta_{MP)}=0\,.

We then conclude that

and
\Sigma_{IJK}=0
, so that

and (

) becomes

Now,

\sigma_{PJM}=\mathchoice{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\sigma_{TUV}=\mathchoice{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.62883pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.30272pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.78764pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{TUV}}}_{{{PJM}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ABC}}}_{{{TUV}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.49013pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.20723pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ABC}}}_{{{TUV}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.49013pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.20723pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ABC}}}_{{{TUV}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.49013pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.20723pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{N}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.44232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.37102pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.12215pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ABC}}}_{{{TUV}\mathchoice{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[18.6229pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[11.49013pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.20723pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\sigma_{ABC}=-\frac{3}{\gamma^{2}}\sigma_{PJM}\,,

as a consequence of the tracelessness of
\sigma_{IJK}
and its ciclicity. We then conclude
\sigma_{PJM}=0
for all
\gamma\in\mathbb{R}
, and the general solution for
S_{IJ}
has the form

S_{IJ}=\eta_{IJ}U_{K}e^{K}

with
U_{\!K}
arbitrary. Plugging this solution into
\mathcal{E}^{(t)}_{KL}=0
of (
5.4
) removes the dependence in
S
and the system becomes the ones studied in
[
13
,
40
]
, where we found the solution
\widehat{\omega}_{IJ}=\accentset{\circ}{\omega}_{IJ}
. Finally, once we plug the solutions for
S
and
\widetilde{\omega}
,
e^{I}
has to satisfy the Einstein equation coming from
\mathfrak{E}^{(t)}_{L}
.

5.4
Presymplectic form

From (
5.5
) and
[
35
,
36
]
, we have

\displaystyle\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}=\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}}+\frac{1}{\gamma}e_{I}\wedge e_{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\widehat{\omega}^{IJ}\,,

\displaystyle\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}=\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\overline{e}^{I}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\overline{e}_{I}\,.

(5.10)

Alternatively, defining the contorsion
C^{IJ}:=\widehat{\omega}^{IJ}-\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{IJ}
, we can write

\displaystyle\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}

\displaystyle=\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}}+\frac{1}{\gamma}e_{I}\wedge e_{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}C^{IJ}+\frac{1}{\gamma}e_{I}\wedge e_{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{IJ}=

\displaystyle=\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}}+\frac{1}{\gamma}e_{I}\wedge e_{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}C^{IJ}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{d}(e_{I}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}e^{I})\,.

The last equality follows from the expression of

\accentset{\circ}{D}e^{I}=0
(since the connection is the LC one, there is no torsion). Gathering the previous equations and using the relative bicomplex framework
[

]
, we obtain

(5.11)

Notice that, off-shell, the HMS and Palatini symplectic potentials are not equal in the relative cohomology due do the term involving
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}C^{IJ}
. Moreover, we see that the relative cohomology class of the HMS symplectic potentials depends on
\gamma
while in the Palatini case, of course, it does not. However,
C=0
on-shell and we obtain, as in the metric case, the expected equivalence over the space of solutions and the independence of
\gamma
.

6
CHARGES IN THE METRIC FORMALISM

6.1
The HMS and Palatini Lagrangian pairs are not equal in relative cohomology

The space of solutions and the symplectic structure of metric-HMS is the same as in metric-Palatini. However, the Lagrangians are not the same at all. In fact, they are not even in the same relative cohomological class so, in principle, the associated charges computed with the CPS algorithm may differ. Indeed, from our previous computations we have that

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}(L^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}}-L^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}})=-\frac{1}{\gamma}\Big(\mathfrak{E}_{\textrm{H-CP}}^{(m)}\Big)^{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern \mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern
