[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}{\sigma}}}}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{d}\Big(\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\iota_{\vec{q}}\mathrm{vol}\Big)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}(\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}}-\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}})-\jmath^{*}(\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}-\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{PT}})=0

giving non-trivial equations of motions. This shows that the HMS Lagrangians and Palatini Lagrangians are not in the same relative cohomology class.

6.2
Definition of
\xi
-charges

In this subsection we quicky summarize the definition of
\xi
-charges (we follow the notations, definitions, and results of
[
38
]
). Consider a pair of Lagrangians
(L,\overline{\ell})
over the space of fields
\mathcal{F}=\{\phi\ \text{tensor field}\}
defining a good action principle. This means that we have

\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}L=E\mathrel{\!{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox to6.66669pt{\hss\,$\wedge$\hss}\hss}\hbox{$\mathrel{\wedge}$}}\!}\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\phi+\mathrm{d}\Theta\qquad\qquad\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\overline{\ell}-\jmath^{*}\Theta=\overline{b}\mathrel{\!{\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox to6.66669pt{\hss\,$\wedge$\hss}\hss}\hbox{$\mathrel{\wedge}$}}\!}\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\phi-\mathrm{d}\overline{\theta}

Given some vector field
\xi^{\alpha}
tangent to
\partial_{L}M
, we define the
\xi
-currents and the
\xi
-charge associated with a Lagrangian pair
(L,\overline{\ell})
as

\begin{array}[]{l}J_{\xi}:=\iota_{\xi}L-\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}\Theta\\
\overline{\jmath}_{\xi}:=-\iota_{\overline{\xi}}\overline{\ell}-\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}\overline{\theta}\end{array}\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathbb{Q}^{\imath}_{\xi}:=\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}(J_{\xi},\overline{\jmath}_{\xi})

where
is the interior product of
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\phi(\mathbb{X}_{\xi})=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\phi
, and
\underline{\imath}:(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)\hookrightarrow(M,\partial_{L}M)
is a Cauchy embedding. The
\xi
-charges depend on the embedding off-shell but are independent on-shell. Moreover, in general they also depend on the Lagrangians chosen within the cohomological class. However, if we only allow Diff-invariant representatives, the charge does not depend on the choice.

Finally, lemma III.54 of
[
38
]
shows that

\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\mathbb{Q}^{\imath}_{\xi}=\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}\includegraphics[height]{./OOmega.pdf}^{\imath}+\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}\Big(\underline{\imath}_{\xi}(E,\overline{b})\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\phi\Big)+\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}(\underline{\mathcal{L}}_{\xi}-\underline{\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}}_{\widetilde{\mathbb{X}}_{\xi}})\big(\Theta,\overline{\theta}\big)

(6.1)

If we have, as we do in our case, Diff-invariant representatives
(\Theta,\overline{\theta})
, the last integral is zero. Meanwhile, if we restrict ourselves to the space of solutions
\raisebox{-1.59302pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./jj.pdf}}:\mathrm{Sol}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{F}
, then the first integral vanishes. Thus we have that
\mathbb{X}_{\xi}|_{\mathrm{Sol}}
is a Hamiltonian vector field with Hamiltonian
\mathbb{Q}_{\xi}:=\raisebox{-1.59302pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./jj.pdf}}^{*}\mathbb{Q}^{\imath}_{\xi}
(
\mathbb{Q}_{\xi}
is said to be integrable).

6.3
HMS vs Palatini vs GR
\xi
-charges

Let us prove that the HMS and Palatini
\xi
-charges coincide and that they are both equal to the
\xi
-charges of GR after pulling back to the metric sector.

From (
3.8
), (
4.2
) and
[
35
]
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}},\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}})&=(\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{PT}},\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{PT}})+\frac{1}{\gamma}\underline{\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}}(\iota_{\vec{q}}\mathrm{vol},0)\\
&=(\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{GR}},\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{GR}})+\underline{\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}}(\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol},0)\end{split}

(6.2)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big(L^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}},\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}}\big)&=(L^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}},\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}})-\frac{1}{\gamma}\Big(\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{H-CP}},0\Big)+\frac{1}{\gamma}\underline{\mathrm{d}}(\iota_{\vec{q}}\mathrm{vol},0)\\
&=\big(L^{(m)}_{\textrm{EH}},\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{GHY}}\big)+\Big(\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{P-CP}}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{H-CP}},0\Big)+\underline{\mathrm{d}}(\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol},0)\end{split}

(6.3)

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{HMS},\imath}_{\xi,(m)}-\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}^{\mathrm{GR},\imath}_{\xi,(m)}=\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}\!\left(\underline{\iota}_{\xi}(L_{\mathrm{HMS}}^{(m)},\overline{\ell}_{\mathrm{HMS}}^{(m)})-\underline{\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}(\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}},\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{HMS}})-\underline{\iota}_{\xi}(L_{\mathrm{EH}}^{(m)},\overline{\ell}_{\mathrm{GHY}}^{(m)})+\underline{\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}(\Theta^{(m)}_{\mathrm{GR}},\overline{\theta}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{GR}})\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}\iota_{\xi}\Big(\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{P-CP}}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{H-CP}}\Big)+\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}\left(\underline{\iota}_{\xi}\underline{\mathrm{d}}(\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol},0)-\underline{\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}\underline{\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}}(\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol},0)\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}\iota_{\xi}\Big(\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{P-CP}}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{H-CP}}\Big)+\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}(\underline{\mathcal{L}}_{\xi}-\underline{\mathrm{d}}\,\underline{\iota}_{\xi})\underline{\iota}_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}(\mathrm{vol},0)-\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}(\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}-\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}})\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}\iota_{\xi}\Big(\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{P-CP}}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{H-CP}}\Big)+\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}-\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}})\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol}=\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}\iota_{\xi}\Big(\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{P-CP}}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\hat{L}^{(m)}_{\textrm{H-CP}}\Big)

To get the last line we have used the relative Stokes’ theorem together with
\underline{\partial}(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)=\varnothing
and the fact that
\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}=0
over
0
-forms over the space of fields. Finally, the last equality follows because
\iota_{\vec{A}-\vec{C}+\vec{q}/\gamma}\mathrm{vol}
does not depend on any background object (over such objects
\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}
). Finally, (see (
3.31
)), we use that the coupling Lagrangians are zero on-shell to prove the equality of the charges.

Notice that we have written
\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{GR},\imath}
, instead of simply
\mathbb{Q}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{GR},\imath}
, to remind the reader that, although they have the same functional expression, they live in different spaces. The former lives in the Palatini/HMS space
\mathcal{F}^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}}(=\mathcal{F}^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}})
while the latter lives in the GR space
\mathcal{F}^{(m)}_{\textrm{GR}}
(they are equal after pullback/projection).

Since
\mathbb{Q}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{HMS}}:=\raisebox{-1.59302pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./jj.pdf}}^{*}\mathbb{Q}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{HMS},\imath}=\raisebox{-1.59302pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./jj.pdf}}^{*}\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}^{\mathrm{GR},\imath}_{\xi,(m)}
does not depend on
\gamma
, we obtain
\mathbb{Q}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{HMS}}=\mathbb{Q}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{PT}}=\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{\xi,(m)}^{\mathrm{PT}}
and all the charges are equivalent (the particular expression is given in
[
36
, III.4]
). Moreover, from (
6.1
) it follows that these charges are the Hamiltonian of
\mathbb{X}_{\xi}

\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}\includegraphics[height]{./OOmega.pdf}_{(m)}^{\mathrm{HMS}}=\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern (6.4)

7
CHARGES IN THE TETRAD FORMALISM

7.1
HMS and Palatini Lagrangian pairs are NOT equal in relative cohomology

We have seen that
(L^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}},\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{HMS}})
and
(L^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}},\overline{\ell}^{(m)}_{\textrm{PT}})
are different in the relative cohomology but, as mentioned on remark
5.2
, this does not imply that the tetrad counterparts
(L^{(t)}_{\textrm{HMS}},\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\textrm{HMS}})
and
(L^{(t)}_{\textrm{PT}},\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\textrm{PT}})
are different in relative cohomology. However, proceeding as in section
6.1
allows us to show that they are different in the relative cohomology as well.

7.2
HMS vs Palatini vs GR
\xi
-charges

Let us prove that the HMS and Palatini
\xi
-charges are equal and that they are both equal, after pulling back to the metric sector, to the
\xi
-charges of GR.

To this end first notice that

\widetilde{\omega}_{IJ}=\accentset{\circ}{\omega}_{IJ}+C_{IJ}+S_{IJ}\,,

The Lagrangian pairs of the three theories are related by

\displaystyle\begin{split}(L^{(t)}_{\textrm{HMS}},\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\textrm{HMS}})&=(L^{(t)}_{\textrm{PT}},\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\textrm{PT}})+\frac{1}{\gamma}\Big(e^{I}\wedge &\hskip 17.22217pt+\frac{1}{\gamma}\underline{\mathrm{d}}\big(e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge C_{IJ},0\big)=\\
&=(L^{(t)}_{\textrm{EH}},\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\textrm{HGY}})+\frac{1}{2}\big(H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge &\hskip 17.22217pt+\frac{1}{2}\underline{\mathrm{d}}\big(H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge C^{KL},0\big)\,,\end{split}

(7.1)

while their symplectic potentials are related by

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}},\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}})&=(\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}},\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}})+\frac{1}{\gamma}(e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}C_{IJ},0)-\frac{1}{\gamma}\underline{\mathrm{d}}(e_{I}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}e^{I},0)\\
&=(\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{GR}},\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{GR}})+\frac{1}{2}(H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}C^{KL},0)-\frac{1}{\gamma}\underline{\mathrm{d}}(e_{I}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}e^{I},0)\,.\end{split}

(7.2)

Using again the relative Stokes’ theorem, the fact that there are no background objects and the relative Cartan’s magic formula, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{HMS},\imath}_{\xi,(t)}-\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}^{\mathrm{GR},\imath}_{\xi,(t)}=\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}\!\left(\underline{\iota}_{\xi}(L_{\mathrm{HMS}}^{(t)},\overline{\ell}_{\mathrm{HMS}}^{(t)})-\underline{\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}(\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}},\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}})-\underline{\iota}_{\xi}(L_{\mathrm{EH}}^{(t)},\overline{\ell}_{\mathrm{GHY}}^{(t)})+\underline{\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}}(\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{GR}},\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{GR}})\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma}\imath^{*}\left(\frac{1}{2}\iota_{\xi}\big(H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge(\mathchoice{C^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{C^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{C^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{C^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}\wedge\mathchoice{C^{{{M}{L}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{{M}{L}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][ }}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}{S^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{M}}}}\wedge\mathchoice{S^{{{M}{L}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{{M}{L}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{{M}{L}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{{M}{L}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.3764pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.2879pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.77707pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})+H_{IJKL}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e^{I})\wedge e^{J}\wedge C^{KL}\right)\,.

On shell,
C_{IJ}=0
and
\mathchoice{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{S^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
is proportional to the identity
\eta^{I}_{J}
. Thus, on shell, the previous expression vanishes and we have once again the equivalence of the charges (the particular expression is given in
[
36
, IV.8]
) and the fact that these charges are the Hamiltonian of
\mathbb{X}_{\xi}
.

Once again, we have reached the expected equivalence between the metric and tetrad formalism. However, it is interesting to note that in the tetrad formalism we have an additional symmetry which is absent in the metric formalism. To introduce it, we need to take a small detour and speak about Kosmann derivatives,
\mathrm{d}
-symmetries, and general charges.

7.3
Kosmann
\xi
-charges

In the metric formalism,
g
-Killing vector fields
\xi\in\mathfrak{X}(M)
satisfy
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}g=0
. If the metric is written in terms of tetrads as
g=\eta_{IJ}e^{I}\otimes e^{J}
, the condition
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}g=0
is equivalent to requiring that
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e^{I}=-\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}e^{J}
for some
\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IJ}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IJ}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IJ}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IJ}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
antisymmetric but non-zero in general. This observation leads to the introduction of the Kosmann derivative
\mathcal{K}_{\xi}e^{I}:=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e^{I}+\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}e^{J}
, choosing
\hat{\lambda}^{IJ}
so that it vanishes for every Killing vector field. The Kosmann derivative has been used, among other things, to study black hole entropy
[
41
]
(see also
[
42
]
for a more geometric discussion).

In the bicomplex formalism, this construction can be defined as follows: consider a
\hat{\lambda}
-dependent vector field
\mathbb{Z}_{\hat{\lambda}}
given by
\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\hat{\lambda}}}e^{I}:=\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}e^{J}
and such that

\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}+\mathbb{Z}_{\hat{\lambda}}}e^{I}=0\qquad\equiv\qquad\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e^{I}+\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}e^{J}=0

(7.3)

for every Killing vector field
\xi
. By demanding that (
7.3
) holds, we can get the explicit form of
\hat{\lambda}^{IJ}
in the following way

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IK}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.05054pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.03609pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IK}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.05054pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.03609pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IK}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.05054pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.03609pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{IK}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.29797pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.05054pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.03609pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{[IK]}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.77277pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.98051pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{[IK]}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.77277pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.98051pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{[IK]}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.77277pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.98051pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{[IK]}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.82578pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.77277pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.98051pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\eta^{J[K}\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}=e_{\alpha}^{J}E^{\alpha[K}\mathchoice{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\hat{\lambda}^{{{I]}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.35211pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}=-E^{\alpha[K}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e^{I]})_{\alpha}=-E^{\alpha[K}\left(\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}_{\vec{\xi}}\,e_{\alpha}^{I]}+e_{\beta}^{I]}\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}_{\alpha}\xi^{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle=\xi^{\beta}e_{\alpha}^{[I}\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}_{\beta}E^{\alpha K]}-E^{\alpha K}E^{\beta I}\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}_{[\alpha}\xi_{\beta]}=\imath_{\xi}\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{IK}-\frac{1}{2}E^{\alpha K}E^{\beta I}(\mathrm{d}\xi)_{\alpha\beta}\,,

where
\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}
is the Levi-Civita connection. With the previous choice of
\hat{\lambda}^{IJ}
, we have:

(\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{X}_{\xi}+\mathbb{Z}_{\hat{\lambda}}}e^{I})_{\alpha}=\frac{1}{2}E^{I\beta}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}g)_{\alpha\beta}

which indeed vanishes if
\xi
is a Killing of the metric.

Now we have to prove that
\mathbb{Z}_{\hat{\lambda}}
is a symmetry and compute the associated charges.

7.4
Definition of
\mathbb{X}
-charges

A
\mathrm{d}
-symmetry is a vector field
\mathbb{X}
over
\mathcal{F}
such that
\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}{X}L
is exact when no boundary is considered. If we assume that the base manifold has a non-empty boundary, then we have to rely on the concept of relative
\mathrm{d}
-symmetry (or
\underline{\mathrm{d}}
-symmetry for short) introduced in
[
38
]
: a vector field
\mathbb{X}
over
\mathcal{F}
such that
\underline{\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}}{X}(L,\overline{\ell})
is relative exact. That means that there exists some
(S{X},\overline{s}{X})
such that

\underline{\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}}{X}(L,\overline{\ell})=\underline{\mathrm{d}}(S{X},\overline{s}{X}):=(\mathrm{d}S{X},\jmath^{*}S{X}-\mathrm{d}\overline{s}{X})\,.

Now we can define the
\mathbb{X}
-currents and
\mathbb{X}
-charge

(J{X},\overline{\jmath}{X}):=(S{X},\overline{s}{X})-\underline{\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}}_{\mathbb{X}}(\Theta,\overline{\theta})\,,\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathbb{Q}^{\imath}_{\mathbb{X}}:=\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}\underline{\imath}^{*}(J{X},\overline{\jmath}{X})\,.

The charge
\mathbb{Q}^{\imath}{X}
is independent of the chosen representatives. Moreover, restricting ourselves to the space of solutions we see that it does not depend on the embedding and it is the Hamiltonian of
\mathbb{X}
. Finally, a comment is in order: if we apply these definitions to
\mathbb{X}_{\xi}
, we recover the ones given in section
6.2
(although notice that those are defined even if
\mathbb{X}_{\xi}
is not a
\underline{\mathrm{d}}
-symmetry).

7.5
Definition of
\lambda
-charges

Now we have to prove that
\mathbb{Z}_{\hat{\lambda}}
is a
\underline{d}
-symmetry. In fact, we are going to prove something stronger, that
\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}
is a
\underline{d}
-symmetry for
every
scalar field
\lambda^{IJ}
antisymmetric in its internal indices. Let us define the vector field
\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}
over
\mathcal{F}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{PT}}
by

\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}e^{I}=\lambda^{I}_{J}e^{J}\,,\qquad\qquad\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}\widehat{\omega}^{IJ}=-\widehat{D}\lambda^{IJ}\,,\qquad\qquad\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}S^{IJ}=-\lambda^{IK}\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\lambda^{JK}\mathchoice{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{S^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

From (
5.1
) and (
5.3
), it is clear that
\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}
is a
\underline{\mathrm{d}}
-symmetry of
(L^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}},\overline{\ell}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}})
and we can take
(S_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}},\overline{s}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}})=(0,0)
. We now compute the
\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}
-currents to obtain

\displaystyle J_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}=-\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}\Theta^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}=\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{d}(H_{IJKL}\lambda^{KL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J})-H_{IJKL}\lambda^{KL}\mathchoice{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}\wedge e^{R}\wedge e^{J}\,,

\displaystyle\overline{\jmath}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}=-\raisebox{-0.17218pt}{\includegraphics[height]{./ii.pdf}}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}\overline{\theta}^{(t)}_{\mathrm{HMS}}=\varepsilon_{IJKL}\lambda^{RJ}\overline{e}^{K}\wedge\overline{e}^{L}N^{I}N_{R}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\lambda^{IJ}\overline{e}_{I}\wedge\overline{e}_{J}\,.

These expressions can be rewritten in a relative form as

\displaystyle(J_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}},\overline{\jmath}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\underline{\mathrm{d}}\big(H_{IJKL}\lambda^{KL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J},0\big)-\big(H_{IJKL}\lambda^{KL}\mathchoice{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}\wedge e^{R}\wedge e^{J},0\big){}+{}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\frac{1}{2}\big(0,\varepsilon_{IJKL}(2N^{I}N_{R}\lambda^{RJ}-\lambda^{IJ})\overline{e}^{K}\wedge\overline{e}^{L}\big)\,.

Finally, notice that the last term is zero as a consequence of
\epsilon_{[IJKL}N_{R]}N^{I}\lambda^{JR}\overline{e}^{K}\wedge\overline{e}^{L}=0
.

We end by computing the
\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}
-charge (
\lambda
-charge for short) as the integral of the
\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}
-currents

\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{HMS},\imath}_{\lambda}:=\int_{(\Sigma,\partial\Sigma)}(J_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}},\overline{\jmath}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}})=-\int_{\Sigma}H_{IJKL}\lambda^{KL}\mathchoice{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}{C^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{R}}}}\wedge e^{R}\wedge e^{J}\,,

which is zero on-shell (since over the space of solutions
C^{IJ}:=\widehat{\omega}^{IJ}-\accentset{\circ}{\omega}^{IJ}
vanishes). Moreover, from (
7.2
) and the relative Stokes’ theorem, we have

\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{HMS},\imath}_{\lambda}=\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{PT},\imath}_{\lambda}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\int_{\Sigma}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}C_{IJ}=\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{GR},\imath}_{\lambda}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma}H_{IJKL}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\wedge\includegraphics[height]{./LL.pdf}_{\mathbb{Z}_{\lambda}}C^{KL}\,.

We see that the
\lambda
-charges do not coincide off-shell in GR, Palatini, and HMS. However, as expected since there is no metric counterpart, they all vanish on-shell.

8
CONCLUSIONS AND COMMENTS

In this paper we have studied in full detail the relation between the metric-HMS and tetrad-HMS formulations for general relativity on manifolds with or without boundary. First we have proven that the spaces of solutions of the metric-HMS action and the metric-Palatini action are the same. Then we have studied the correspondence between the solution spaces in the metric and tetrad formalisms. Although the simple relationship between them can be justified on general grounds by relying on the properties of the transformation
\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
and its tangent map (in particular, by the fact that both are onto), we have checked this explicitly by solving the relevant sector of the field equations. In order to do this, we have used the irreducible decompositions of the tensors involved. We would like to insist on several facts:

•

We have done this in full generality, i.e. by taking from the start complete general connections with torsion and non metricity. In particular, in the tetrad formalism we have the Holst action plus another term that depends on the symmtric part of the connection. To the best of our knoweledge, this has not been considered before.

•

We have derived a new boundary Lagrangian to recover GR also at the boundary, and used the transformation
\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
to find its tetrad counterpart. The latter coincides with the boundary Lagrangian proposed in
[
37
]
by Bodendorfer and Neiman (although they only work with Lorentz connections).

•

As expected, the equivalence of the solution spaces extends to the case of manifolds with boundaries.

As far as the (pre)symplectic forms are concerned the situation is very simple in the metric case as the symplectic potential corresponding to the different formulations (Einstein-Hilbert, Palatini, and HMS) differ by a
\mathchoice{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{6.91037pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.1873pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.1873pt\makebox[6.91037pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.1873pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{4.84373pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.17221pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.17221pt\makebox[4.84373pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.17221pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}{\mathbbm{d}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{3.44444pt}\raisebox{0.0pt}{\kern 0.12915pt\rotatebox{90.0}{\kern-0.12915pt\makebox[3.44444pt][l]{\pdfliteral}}\kern 0.12915pt}\hskip 0.6458pt}
-exact term. In fact, they coincide both off-shell and on-shell. The tetrad case is more complicated. This is to be expected on general grounds because the transformation
\Phi_{\mathrm{HMS}}
involves derivatives. As we have shown, the HMS and Palatini symplectic potentials are not equal on the relative cohomology: they are different off-shell but coincide on-shell as a consequence of the dynamical vanishing of the contortion
C_{IJ}
.

Finally, regarding the charges we have shown that they also differ off-shell, but coincide on shell (again as a consequence of the fact that
C_{IJ}=0
). A similar analysis has been performed for the
\lambda
-charges (which include the Kosmann charges) proving that, in fact, they all vanish.
